**Asker:** Abullish (`nft_spaceman`) in #💬-stonks-yapping-💬

**Route:** `LOCAL/BANTER` · grounded ✅ (7 sources) · guards: —

**Q:** What percent are semis and memory stocks of qqq and voo

**A:**

→ **QQQ weight:** Pure semiconductor and memory components (including **NVIDIA**, **Micron**, **AMD**, **Broadcom**, **Intel**, and **Applied Materials**) make up roughly **25% to 28%** of the total Nasdaq-100 index, driving the core of its broader ~54% technology allocation.

→ **VOO weight:** Semiconductor stocks account for roughly **15% to nearly 19%** of the entire S&P 500 market cap, heavily anchored by giants like **NVIDIA** (~7.3%) and **Broadcom** (~2.6%), making chips the single largest industry group in the index.

→ **Memory specifics:** Pure memory players like **Micron (MU)** hold about a **4.7%** share inside QQQ and roughly **2%** in VOO, giving memory cycles outsized influence whenever institutional desks rotate out of hardware beta.

Sources:
[1] [slickcharts.com](<https://vertexaisearch.cloud.google.com/grounding-api-redirect/AUZIYQHx4qcsTO7W2BLV3Rkgr3bSmYsuA8l26AQOYkwQHDVw3kiE-T4ehmftgJ48jef6f48d_n5KnuUnfmEZwOA5E_reoCvkGt_no-OcFX146ATjgul4EoBsCcafm242PRi9zEZsTjRmbg==>)
